68:Tde0,
## What

Adds `packages/agent/src/services/knowledge-graph/__tests__/relationship-store.semantics.test.ts`, a new unit suite for `RelationshipStore`. Test-only change: one new file, +473/-0, no production code touched.

The existing `relationship-store.test.ts` covers persistence, list filters, observe strengthen-or-create, and retire/audit basics. This suite pins five adjacent observable behaviors it does not cover:

1. **Parallel active edges.** When two active edges share an `(from, to, type)` triple (the store does not enforce uniqueness on that triple), `observe` strengthens only the most recently updated edge; the older twin is byte-identical before and after.
2. **Metadata patch depth.** An observe `metadataPatch` shallow-replaces nested objects (`profile: {city:"ny"}` replaces the whole previous `profile`, dropping `zip`) while keeping sibling keys — the spread-merge semantics callers get.
3. **Cadence-overdue boundary.** The overdue filter is inclusive at exactly `lastInteractionAt + cadenceDays` and excludes one millisecond earlier, including when `cadenceDays` first arrives through an observe patch.
4. **Repeated retirement + audit scoping.** Retiring twice overwrites `retiredReason`/`retiredAt`, appends a second ordered `retire` audit event, preserves `createdAt`, and audit events are scoped to the owning agent id.
5. **Evidence dedupe asymmetry.** The create path stores repeated evidence verbatim (`["d1","d1"]`), while the strengthen path Set-deduplicates across and within observations.

## How it is tested

The suite drives the real `RelationshipStore` against an in-memory interpreter of the SQL shapes the store emits (same harness approach as the base suite); drizzle `sql.raw` stays real and no production helper is replaced with a mock of itself. No `vi.mock`, no `expectTypeOf`; assertions are runtime behavior only.

## Evidence (fork contribution — hosted CI does not run on this PR)

- `bunx vitest run packages/agent/src/services/knowledge-graph/__tests__/relationship-store.semantics.test.ts` against current `develop` tip (`1f236fd1f58f`): **1 file passed, 5 tests passed**.
- Run together with the pre-existing base suite: **2 files passed, 33 tests passed** (28 pre-existing + 5 new) — the two suites coexist with no collection conflicts.

## Background

### What does this PR do?

Adds `packages/agent/src/runtime/roles/src/index.test.ts`, a deterministic unit suite for the roles plugin bootstrap that previously had no same-named test file on `develop`. The diff is a single new test file: +462/-0, no production code touched.

The suite drives the real module (`rolesPlugin.init()` and its exported surface) against a scripted `IAgentRuntime` seam — no mocks of the module under test:

- **Owner bootstrap:** grants OWNER to the configured app user; idempotent no-write when already consistent; demotes every other OWNER only when a canonical owner is configured; syncs a recorded world owner without configuration while keeping other owners; no-ops when no owner resolves; skips id-less worlds; survives worlds disappearing before the metadata update.
- **Connector-admin whitelists:** promotes whitelisted entities to ADMIN with source `connector_admin`; revokes stale grants that no longer match; never promotes entities that already hold a role; loads whitelists from the settings JSON; treats malformed settings JSON as "no whitelist" and prunes stale grants through the owner pass; prefers direct plugin config over the settings JSON.
- **Deferred retries:** schedules the owner-bootstrap retry (label `Owner role bootstrap`) when runtime worlds are unavailable, re-attempts after the first backoff window, and stops after the third failed retry (initial call + 3 retries observed, timer map empty afterwards).
- **World-event hooks:** registers exactly `WORLD_JOINED` / `WORLD_CONNECTED`; invoking the hook re-applies owner bootstrap to a world created after init, and the second hook is a no-op write once state is already consistent (idempotent re-run).
- **Surface:** plugin exposes `rolesProvider` and `roleAction`; `ROLE_RANK` is strictly monotonic GUEST < USER < ADMIN < OWNER.

### What kind of change is this?

Test-only coverage addition. No behavior change, no dependency change, no public surface change.

## What

Adds `packages/ui/src/chat/useSlashCommandController.side-effects.test.ts` — a new, complementary vitest suite for `useSlashCommandController`. Test-only: **1 new file, +459/−0**, no production code touched.

## Why

Current develop already carries two suites for this hook (`useSlashCommandController.catalog.test.ts`, `useSlashCommandController.models.test.ts`). This suite covers the behavior they do not reach:

- **Merged-catalog composition** — server catalog wins alias collisions (case-insensitively) against saved commands and custom actions; locally-derived saved (localStorage-seeded through the real `loadSavedCustomCommands` path) and enabled custom-action commands still render when the server catalog is empty; disabled custom actions are excluded.
- **App-level side effects** — `navigateTab` switches the shell tab and reports `{ source: "user" }` with no path key; `navigateSettings` dispatches `NAVIGATE_SETTINGS_EVENT` with the section detail and reports it as a settings view path; `navigateView` dispatches the real `NAVIGATE_VIEW_EVENT` on window and reports only when a `viewId` is present (bare deep-links navigate unreported); `clearChat` delegates to the shell handler; `openCommandPalette` fires the palette event on document; `naturalShortcutsEnabled` stays `false`.
- **Telemetry guards (#8792)** — no report fetch without a configured API base; Authorization header omitted when no token; a synchronously failing base read never throws; a non-ok report response degrades to `logger.warn` for both reporters instead of breaking navigation.
- **Choice sources beyond "models"** — view ids from the live registry, the shared `SETTINGS_SECTION_SUGGESTIONS` passed through by reference, unwired sources answer empty, and `resolveSection` maps typed tokens to canonical section ids.

Assertions drive the real module (real events bus, real saved-command storage parsing, stubbed only at the network boundary), matching the house rule of asserting observed behavior rather than mock echoes.

## Evidence

- `bunx vitest run src/chat/useSlashCommandController.side-effects.test.ts` (packages/ui, vitest 4.1.10 jsdom): **1 file passed, 15 tests passed**, 0 failed.
- `bunx biome check src/chat/useSlashCommandController.side-effects.test.ts`: clean ("Checked 1 file ... No fixes applied").
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` in `packages/ui`: zero diagnostics mention the new file; total package error count is unchanged versus an untouched control run (14 pre-existing errors elsewhere, none added).

## Summary

Adds real unit coverage for `packages/agent/src/api/waifu-chat-role-resolver.ts` by appending 20 new cases to the existing suite at `packages/agent/test/api/waifu-chat-role-resolver.test.ts`. The diff is purely additive (+458/-0): no existing case is modified or removed.

The suite already on `develop` proves parity, the boundary-role extension point, and the frozen role map. This change fills gaps it does not yet exercise, all driven through the real module (real HMAC-signed JWTs, no mocks):

**JWT claim validation**
- array-form `aud` containing `eliza-cloud-chat` accepted; without it rejected
- `nbf` in the future rejected; at/before now accepted
- `exp` exactly equal to the current second rejected
- wallet fallback from `sub` (trimmed); whitespace-trimmed `walletAddress`
- non-40-hex / missing wallet identities rejected
- `TOKEN_CHAIN_ID` enforcement: mismatched, string-typed, and absent `chainId` claims rejected
- missing/whitespace-only secret rejected
- structurally malformed tokens (empty, wrong segment count, empty signature segment, unparsable header) rejected before claim inspection
- correctly signed token whose header declares a non-HS256 `alg` still rejected
- role claims outside `admin|user|guest` (including case variants) rejected
- `balanceTokens` carried verbatim, including explicit `null`, numeric, and key-omitted forms
- `ELIZA_CLOUD_AGENT_ID` fallback binding when `WAIFU_ELIZA_CLOUD_AGENT_ID` is unset

**Route scoping + transport extraction**
- full method/path matrix over every chat-safe route for non-admin principals (18 combinations)
- HTTP method case-insensitivity (`"post"`/`"get"`/`"delete"`)
- admin bypass of the scoped-route allowlist
- requests with no recognizable token header resolve no access
- tokens accepted from `x-eliza-token`, `x-elizaos-token`, `x-waifu-chat-access-token`, and `x-api-key` fallback headers; malformed fallback tokens fail closed
- `Authorization: Bearer` takes precedence over fallback headers

**Registration semantics**
- re-registering the same resolver id replaces rather than double-stacks; stale unregister handles are harmless no-ops
- the registered resolver defers (`null`) for requests carrying no waifu token

# Change

`normalizeType` in the runtime-migrator diff calculator only canonicalized a
handful of spellings, so equivalent Postgres column types registered as schema
changes and the migrator emitted drop-and-recreate DDL for no-op migrations:

- `int`/`int4` → `integer`, `int2` → `smallint`, `int8` → `bigint`
- `bool` → `boolean`
- `float4` → `real`, `float8` → `double precision`
- `timestamptz` and `timestamp(p) with/without time zone` → `timestamp(p)`
  (the bare `timestamp`/`timestamp with time zone` pair was already folded)
- `numeric(p)` → `numeric(p,0)` — a missing scale defaults to `0` in Postgres

Each alias is pinned by a focused unit test, and genuine changes
(`integer`→`text`, `numeric(10,2)`→`numeric(12,2)`, varchar length,
nullability, index/FK/constraint shape) still produce diffs.

# Risks

Low. The change only widens type normalization to spellings Postgres treats
as identical; tests cover both the alias-equivalence cases and the
still-diffing genuine-change cases.

6e:Tff9,
## Summary

Adds a new unit test suite for the connector source registry in `packages/core/src/connectors.ts` (`connectors.test.ts`). This is a test-only change: no production source file is modified, and the diff is a single new file (`+419/-0`).

## What the suite covers

The registry canonicalizes inbound message `source` tags (discord, telegram, ...) through owner-scoped alias/metadata registrations. The 31 cases drive the real module directly (no mocks) and pin its observable contracts:

- `normalizeConnectorSource`: nullish/non-string/blank inputs return `""`; unregistered sources return their trimmed lowercase form; registered aliases resolve to canonical; lookups are case- and whitespace-insensitive.
- Registration semantics: the canonical key always joins its own alias set; aliases accumulate across repeated registrations; incoming aliases are trimmed/lowercased/deduped; blank canonical keys are a no-op; whitespace-only owners default to `manual`; same-owner re-registration preserves fields it omits.
- Owner scoping: contributions merge per owner (alias union across owners); scalar fields from the later-registered owner win with fallback on teardown; unregistering an owner removes only that owner's data; blank/unknown owners are ignored.
- Legacy Discord backstop: the module-load registration exposes the documented `{ userIdField: "fromId", nameField: "entityName" }` identity projection and ordered world-id keys, and is restored after an overriding plugin owner unregisters (the documented back-compat contract).
- Classification and projections: passive detection via either `isPassive` or `sourceKind === "passive"`; identity mappings reject missing/blank/non-string `userIdField`, trim declared fields, and omit a blank `nameField`; world-id key lists drop non-string/blank entries while preserving order.
- Filter expansion: `expandConnectorSourceFilter` returns an empty set for nullish input and expands each entry to canonical plus aliases, deduped across entries.

One observed behavior is recorded as implemented rather than as first guessed: for any non-empty unknown source, `getConnectorSourceMetadata` returns an empty metadata shell (empty alias list, undefined fields) rather than `null`; `null` is reserved for nullish input. The assertions record exactly what the code does.

## Evidence (test-only change)

- `bunx vitest run packages/core/src/connectors.test.ts` → **Test Files 1 passed (1); Tests 31 passed (31)** — re-fetched and re-run immediately before filing against current `origin/develop` (`de774b875774f478ef5b879dbdae8fd2997a3470`, which is this branch's base).
- `bunx biome check packages/core/src/connectors.test.ts` → clean ("Checked 1 file... No fixes applied").
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` in `packages/core`: the new test file appears nowhere in the output (zero type errors introduced).
- The diff touches only the new test file `packages/core/src/connectors.test.ts`.

Note: we contribute from a fork, so hosted CI does not run on this pull request; the counts above are quoted from local runs of the exact head commit.

6f:T1eed,## Summary

Closes #22956 (implementation slice: fail-closed missing-catalogue behavior + exact-decimal/fail-closed tests; checkboxes 1, 3, 7 — price ratification, docs/ledger alignment, effective-date policy — remain product-owner decisions and are intentionally untouched).

Storage bills **mixed units**: a PUT performs two catalogue lookups (flat per-request `put` + per-byte `put_per_byte` multiplied by content length); DELETE is seeded at $0. When the pricing catalogue is empty (zero rows), the generic per-call `$0.001` fallback was billed **per component**: an empty-catalogue PUT billed `$0.001 + $0.001 × bytes` — a per-request fallback multiplied as a per-byte price — and the free DELETE silently billed `$0.001`.

The fix adds `requireServiceMethodCost`, a fail-closed lookup for mixed-unit services: any missing row (empty **or** partial catalogue) throws `PricingNotFoundError`, which each storage route boundary maps to a retryable `503 pricing_unavailable` (Retry-After: 30) **before any debit or provider effect**. All seven storage lookups migrate (`put`, `put_per_byte`, `get`, `head`, `delete`, `list`, `presign`). The generic `getServiceMethodCost` fallback — a pinned design decision for single-unit per-call proxy services (#10269, fail-safe undercharge, never cache the empty map) — is deliberately unchanged.

## Root cause (verified at develop `7d55350499`)

`packages/cloud/shared/src/lib/services/proxy/pricing.ts`: `getServiceMethodCost` returns `FALLBACK_COST` (0.001) when the service's pricing map is empty. Storage routes called it for both PUT legs and every other verb, so a missing catalogue billed unratified wrong-unit prices instead of refusing.

## Behavior change

| Scenario (storage catalogue) | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| Empty catalogue, PUT N bytes | 201/`$0.001 + $0.001×N` billed | 503 `pricing_unavailable`, no debit, no provider write |
| Empty catalogue, GET/HEAD/list/presign | 200/`$0.001` billed | 503, no debit, no provider read |
| Empty catalogue, DELETE (seeded free) | 204/`$0.001` billed | 503, no debit, no provider delete |
| Partial catalogue (any storage row missing) | threw `PricingNotFoundError` → 500 | 503 `pricing_unavailable`, no debit |
| Corrupt (non-finite) row | threw plain Error → 500 | unchanged (generic boundary mapping) |

## Tests

- `packages/cloud/shared/.../pricing.error-policy.test.ts`: +7 cases — empty catalogue throws (never fallback), partial catalogue throws for missing row while present row resolves exact decimal, seeded zero row resolves `0` (free ≠ missing), non-finite throws, generic fallback contract unchanged for single-unit services, self-heal (seeded row picked up on the very next call after an empty read; empty map never cached).
- `packages/cloud/api/__tests__/storage-put-body-budget.test.ts`: +2 cases — PUT refused 503 on empty catalogue (no provider put, no debit) and on partial catalogue (`put` present, `put_per_byte` missing) proving a flat-only price can never bill.
- `storage-objects-head-routing.test.ts`: +2 cases — GET/HEAD/DELETE each refuse 503 with per-verb method verification (DELETE provably looks up `storage.delete`), and a corrupt non-finite price refuses the read before provider access.
- `storage-list-native-authority.test.ts`, `storage-presign-contract.test.ts`: +1 case each — 503 before debit/provider enumeration or capability mint.
- **RED control**: with production files reverted to pristine develop (tests kept), all five touched suites fail; at head all pass (33 api + 15 shared).

## Verification

- `bun test` on all five touched suites: **48/48 pass** at head `4a01de8fdf`.
- `bun run --cwd packages/cloud/shared typecheck`: pass. `packages/cloud/api typecheck`: fails on a **pre-existing** baseline error (`@elizaos/plugin-doordash` TS2307 in `shared/src/lib/eliza/runtime/initializer.ts`) — proven identical on pristine `origin/develop` via stash control; no new errors from this change.
- `biome check` on all nine changed files: clean (auto-formatted).
- Independent RepoPrompt review (gpt-5.6-sol, embedded exact-bytes diff + full pre-change pricing.ts): **APPROVE**, zero blocking findings; all three low-severity test-hardening suggestions implemented in `4a01de8fdf` (per-verb DELETE row verification, self-heal strengthening, corrupt-price route refusal).
- Blast radius verified by `git grep` over `origin/develop`: exactly three route modules call storage-method pricing (`objects/[...key]`, `list`, `presign`); all migrated. All other `getServiceMethodCost` consumers (birdeye, dexscreener, chain-data, market-data, evm-rpc, solana-rpc) are single-unit per-call services and deliberately keep the generic fallback.

70:T30a1,
## What

Adds a new unit suite for `packages/ui/src/voice/voice-chat-audio-playback.ts` — the shared decoded-TTS playback lifecycle that every Web Audio TTS provider path goes through. The module previously had no test coverage anywhere in the repo.

## Why

This module owns load-bearing playback orchestration: generation gating against stale clips (before and after an async decode), analyser/source graph wiring, the shared playback-reference tap lifecycle (`PlaybackTapLifecycle`), guarded teardown whose ref-clears must only fire when the refs still hold *this* task's values, the double-finish guard, and the speech-timeout fallback that keeps a wedged `onended` from hanging a voice turn forever. None of these branches were covered by any existing suite.

## How

The module under test runs real end to end. The only doubles are structural fakes standing in for browser-only boundaries: Web Audio graph nodes (a plain recording object graph) and the optional frame-pump tap. Timeout math is proven with fake timers; everything else runs on real microtask/macrotask scheduling.

Coverage:

- returns before decoding when the generation is already stale (no decode call, no graph nodes)
- abandons the clip when the generation goes stale during decode
- wires source -> analyser -> destination with fftSize 2048 / smoothing 0.8 and publishes the analyser, time-domain buffer, and source refs
- emits the playback-start event with task telemetry spread into it (`messageId` etc.)
- starts the tap once playback begins and stops it with `{ reset: true }` on finish
- natural-end teardown: clears speech timers, disconnects both nodes, clears its own active-task-finish and audio-source refs
- leaves a foreign `activeTaskFinishRef` untouched (identity guard)
- leaves a foreign `audioSourceRef` untouched (same-source guard)
- treats a second finish invocation as a no-op
- degrades to audible-only playback when tap attachment rejects (error-policy J4 path)
- speech timeout fires at exactly `duration * 1000 + 1200` ms (proven at 3199 vs 3200 ms)
- short clips are held open for at least the 2500 ms floor (proven at 2499 vs 2500 ms)

## Evidence

## Summary

Adds a deterministic unit suite for `packages/ui/src/bridge/plugin-bridge.ts`, which had no co-located test on `develop`. This is a **test-only change**: one new file, no production code touched (+449/−0).

The suite drives the real module through its actual seams — a controlled `@capacitor/core` double (the same pattern `native-plugins.test.ts` on `develop` establishes) plus real `globalThis` window/navigator probes for the Web Speech, media devices, geolocation, and Electrobun detection paths:

- **`getPluginCapabilities`** — full web-baseline capability object; voice-wake/talk-mode gating on real Web Speech presence (continuous listening stays native-only); system TTS derived from `speechSynthesis`; camera from `getUserMedia`; screen recording added only by `getDisplayMedia` while `screenshot` stays native-only; geolocation without native gps/background; the Electrobun runtime marker (`__electrobunWindowId` and `__electrobunWebviewId`) unlocking gateway discovery and the desktop quad; Android-native matrix including the observed asymmetry that `backgroundLocation` is true while bare `location` stays false without a geolocation provider; iOS withholding the phone/contacts/messages/system slots that are gated on `platform === "android"`.
- **`isFeatureAvailable`** — the full 13-feature switch under the web baseline and the Android matrix, plus ElevenLabs remaining available across web, Android, and desktop runtimes.
- **`getPlugins`** — singleton identity while the desktop state is stable, instance rebuild when the Electrobun marker appears and again when it disappears, exact slot key list, build-time capability snapshot semantics (cached instance keeps stale capabilities until a forced rebuild), wrapper flags stamped from platform and capabilities.
- **plugin wiring / proxy wrapping** — non-function property passthrough through `wrapPlugin`, and method calls keeping their receiver after detachment (destructured `getConfig()` still resolves against the target), using a registry-injected plugin object.

## Evidence

Test-only diff; no production behaviour changed. Counts from the run at this head (sources identical to `origin/develop` @ `1f236fd1f5`):

- `bunx vitest run src/bridge/plugin-bridge.test.ts` (packages/ui): **1 file passed, 17 tests passed**, 0 failed (~170–230 ms).
- `bunx biome check --write src/bridge/plugin-bridge.test.ts`: clean — "Checked 1 file … No fixes applied".
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` in `packages/ui`: zero diagnostics referencing `plugin-bridge.test.ts`.
- `git diff origin/develop..HEAD`: exactly one file added, insertions only.

Note: we are a fork contributor, so hosted CI does not run on this pull request; the counts above are quoted from local runs of the package's own vitest config.

<!-- eliza-computer-attribution:v1 {"provider":"opencode","model":"ox-alpha","client":"opencode","skill_revision":"SlopDotCash/slopdotcash@b549c19dcfd4a12483ec4f8d98e9418a0475c8a8:skills/contribute-to-eliza"} -->
72:T2c84,
Adds a co-located unit suite for `packages/ui/src/cloud-ui/runtime/navigation.ts`, which has no test coverage anywhere in the repository.

The suite drives the real shim through a real `MemoryRouter` (react-router-dom v7) using `@testing-library/react` render/renderHook probes and asserts consumer-visible outcomes only:

- internal pushes route through react-router to the target path; external-origin hrefs go to `window.location.assign` and leave the router untouched
- a same-origin absolute URL normalizes down to path + query + hash before navigation
- an unparseable href never escapes to the browser: it stays on the in-app router, passed through raw
- scroll-to-top fires by default after internal navigation, honours `{ scroll: false }`, and never fires for external hops
- internal `replace()` yields REPLACE navigation type; external `replace()` uses `window.location.replace`
- `refresh` / `back` / `forward` delegate to window reload / browser history
- `prefetch()` resolves as a no-op without navigating
- `notFound()` throws its documented SPA-runtime error; `redirect()` assigns the browser location before throwing
- `useSelectedLayoutSegments` / `useSelectedLayoutSegment` derive segments from live router state (nested path, trailing slash, root)
- `useParams`, `useSearchParams`, and `usePathname` surface real router values through the shim
- `useCallbackRouterPush` returns a referentially stable callback while its href binding is unchanged and rebinds when it changes; invoking it performs the real navigation
- the router object itself is memo-stable across unrelated re-renders

Verification (this is a fork pull request — hosted CI does not run on it):

- `bunx vitest run src/cloud-ui/runtime/navigation.test.tsx` in `packages/ui`: 1 test file passed, **26 passed (26)**, re-run against current `origin/develop` (`1f236fd1f58f`)
- `bunx biome check src/cloud-ui/runtime/navigation.test.tsx`: clean — "Checked 1 file … No fixes applied" (repo `biome.json` present, tree not sparse)
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` in `packages/ui`: no diagnostic mentions the new file
- the diff is exactly one new test file, +447/−0; no production behaviour is changed

73:T10a8,## Summary

Adds unit coverage for the pure boot helpers in `packages/core/src/runtime-composition.ts` — the shared building blocks every host (daemon, cloud, CLI) uses to load characters, flatten basic-capabilities settings, and merge DB-backed settings. The module had zero direct tests on develop; `createRuntimes` remains out of scope (requires DB adapters).

**New file:** `packages/core/src/runtime-composition.test.ts` (+441 lines, 34 tests, test-only — no production changes)

### Covered contracts

- **`getBasicCapabilitiesSettings`** — the documented flatten order with every adjacent link pinned by a pairwise collision (env < settings, settings < settings.secrets, settings.secrets < secrets — no reorder can stay green), stringification of non-string values, null/undefined filtering, nested-secrets deferral, process.env fallback.
- **`mergeSettingsInto`** — DB base with character override, the four-source secrets merge with pairwise precedence keys, higher-precedence null killing a key instead of falling back, all-filtered case, stringification, non-mutation of the input character.
- **`loadCharacters`** — empty sources, inline objects, deterministic id derivation via `stringToUuid` (distinct per name), explicit-id preservation, relative-path resolution via `cwd`, absolute paths ignoring `cwd`, mixed sources in order, missing-file error, and the full `ElizaError` wrapping contract (`CHARACTER_SOURCE_LOAD_FAILED` code, resolved `context.source`, preserved `cause` — `SyntaxError` for broken JSON, non-SyntaxError for schema failures).

## Summary

Adds a new unit suite for `packages/homepage/src/lib/api/siws.ts`, which had no same-named test file anywhere on `develop`.

The merged `tests/siws-hung-http.test.ts` already covers the HTTP consumption boundary (timeouts, media types, byte limits, redirect rejection). This suite is purely additive and covers surface that no existing homepage suite exercises:

- **`bs58Encode`** (exported pure function): empty input, leading-zero `"1"` prefixes, reference vectors (`"hello world"`, `[255,254,253]`), and stable 64-byte signature-payload encodings. Every hardcoded vector was independently cross-checked against the `bs58` npm package before being asserted.
- **Outgoing request construction**: nonce GET URL/query/headers and verify POST body — including the exact signed SIWS message template (with a parsed `Issued At` timestamp check) and the base58-encoded signature matching the signer's bytes.
- **Relying-party validation**: loopback `http://` acceptance for `localhost` / `127.0.0.1` / `[::1]`; rejection of plain-http non-loopback, embedded credentials, and fragments before wallet signing.
- **Nonce schema guards**: `chainId`/`version` contract pins, statement byte budget at the exact 512-byte boundary, statement line-break rejection.
- **Verification cross-checks**: verified address and canonical wallet address must match the signer; API-key budget enforced at the exact 8192-byte boundary.
- **Wallet detection**: no-wallet guidance message; direct signing with an already-connected Phantom (no `connect()` call); nested `window.phantom.solana`; locked-wallet connect flow; rejected connection error.

All cases drive the real `signInWithSolana` / `bs58Encode` through deterministic fetch/wallet doubles with explicit global restoration in `afterEach` (same pattern as the existing sibling suite). No network access occurs.

76:Tce1,
## What

Adds `packages/registry/src/first-party/loader.test.ts` — a new unit suite for
`packages/registry/src/first-party/loader.ts`, which had no same-named test
file anywhere in the repository. Test-only change: no production code is
touched.

## Coverage

The suite drives the real module against the real Zod schemas (no mocks):

- **`loadRegistryFromRawEntries`** — validates mixed-kind raw entries and
  indexes them on every axis; preserves raw input order in `all`; returns
  empty indexes with all three kind keys pre-seeded for an empty list; throws
  `RegistryValidationError` naming the offending file when an entry fails
  schema validation; blames the second file and names the duplicated id when
  two entries share an id.
- **`indexEntries`** — indexes by id/kind/group/npm name while preserving
  entry object identity; omits entries without an `npmName` from `byNpmName`;
  resolves a shared npm name to the last indexed entry; keeps empty buckets
  for kinds with no entries; sorts each group bucket by `groupOrder`, then by
  name on ties, with a missing `groupOrder` falling back to last place;
  never reorders the caller's `all` list when sorting buckets.
- **Kind-narrowed accessors** (`getApps` / `getPlugins` / `getConnectors`) —
  partition a loaded registry by kind and return empty arrays (not undefined)
  for absent kinds.
- **`getEntry` / `getEntryByNpmName`** — resolve hits and return `undefined`
  for misses.
- **`mergeWithRuntime`** — applies each overlay to the matching entry by id
  regardless of array order while keeping static fields; substitutes the safe
  default overlay for entries without runtime state; carries validation
  errors/warnings through the view; returns fresh view objects without
  mutating the input entries.

# Risks

Medium — adds production-capable Redis transport methods, but this slice does not yet switch the gateway routing caller.

# Background

## What does this PR do?

Implements the purpose-bound routing reader on every gateway Redis transport without changing legacy operations.

- Native Redis calls `eval_ro(script, 3, ...keys)` exactly and has no mutating `EVAL` fallback.
- Upstash calls `evalRo(readOnlyScript, keys, [])` with automatic deserialization disabled.
- Raw GET preserves the difference between a missing key and the literal stored value `"null"`.
- The in-memory `EVAL` implementation exists only behind `MOCK_REDIS=1`.
- Hermetic transport tests inspect the actual Upstash command payload.
- Real `ioredis-mock` integration covers the nested content-addressed endpoint, including its canonical `runtimeAgentId`, managed selection despite a legacy pointer, double-snapshot confirmation, and unmanaged legacy resolution.

## What kind of change is this?

Bug fix and gateway routing adapter foundation.

## Summary

Extends the existing unit suite for `packages/examples/code/src/lib/model-provider.ts` with **10 new cases** covering branches the current suite does not reach. The diff is strictly additive: **+114 / −0**, one test file, no production code touched.

## New coverage

- `resolveModelProvider`: a present-but-empty `ELIZA_CODE_PROVIDER` does **not** fall through to `ELIZA_CODE_MODEL_PROVIDER` (`??` only skips nullish values) and auto-detects instead; a whitespace-only explicit value behaves as unset; an unrecognized explicit value with no resolvable key still throws the actionable configuration error.
- `applyElizaCodeProviderEnv`: whitespace-only `ELIZA_CODE_*` sources count as missing across every mapping (the suite previously proved this only for empty strings); whitespace-only `OPENAI_*` targets are overwritten on every mapping (previously proven only for `OPENAI_API_KEY`); the helper mutates the caller's record in place and returns nothing.
- `describeActiveModel`: the anthropic side of the blank-large-model mask (`ANTHROPIC_LARGE_MODEL=""` hides `ANTHROPIC_SMALL_MODEL`, symmetric to the proven openai case); a blank large model beats the small-model fallback chain; labels resolve through the orchestrator-forwarded `ELIZA_CODE_API_KEY`.
- Composition: `applyElizaCodeProviderEnv` followed by `describeActiveModel` reproduces the real orchestrator-to-status-bar handoff for an env filled purely from `ELIZA_CODE_*` vars.

All expectations record observed behaviour of the module as it exists on develop; nothing aspirational. Additive-only onto the suite introduced by #26295 — no existing line is modified or removed.

## What

Adds `packages/core/src/integrations/managed-provider/index.test.ts` — a new vitest suite (409 added lines, single new file, no production changes) covering the managed-provider **SDK facade barrel** that integration plugins import from `./index.ts`.

The sibling suites (`connection`-driven cases in `http-client.test.ts`, `errors.test.ts`, `health.test.ts`, `pagination.test.ts`, `managed-provider.test.ts`) exercise the underlying modules through their own import paths; nothing on develop drove the composed public surface those plugins actually consume. This suite imports exclusively from `./index` and proves the re-exported SDK composes over one resolved connection:

- connection resolution through the facade: managed mode resolves to the gateway origin with no credential key present, local mode keeps its bearer credential, malformed provider ids and non-opaque connection ids reject as `INVALID_INPUT`, and `isOpaqueConnectionId` grammar edges (16-char minimum, missing prefix, rejected symbols);
- client guardrails via the facade: a managed connection forged to carry a credential is refused, and the exported timeout/byte constants are ordered bounds;
- one local connection drives real requests end to end over an injected in-memory fetch: bearer authorization plus `x-eliza-connection-id` headers on the pinned origin URL, payload decoded through a caller schema;
- a live 429 response is classified `RATE_LIMITED`, narrowed by the facade's own `isManagedProviderError`, carries severity `ephemeral` and parsed `retryAfterMs` (3000 ms), and maps onto `rate_limited` in the capability execution contract; an origin escape classifies `ENDPOINT_BLOCKED` and maps to `unknown_error`;
- health probing rides the same resolved managed connection: healthy snapshot against the gateway `/health` default path, and a provider 503 degrades into a fatal `PROVIDER_FAILURE` snapshot mapped to `provider_error`;
- cursor pagination composes with the guarded client: a two-page cursor chain drains in order, and a cursor loop surfaces `MALFORMED_RESPONSE` mapped to `schema_drift`;
- error narrowing discriminates taxonomy instances from base `ElizaError`, plain errors, and non-errors.

All 14 cases drive real behavior — no mock-echo assertions, no `vi.hoisted`, no type-level assertions.

7a:T2e68,
## What

Adds `packages/ui/src/__tests__/stories-concepts-neon.test.ts` — unit coverage for the neon orb concept (`packages/ui/stories/src/concepts/neon.ts`). Test-only change: no production file is touched, diff is exactly one new file (+408/-0).

## Why

`neon.ts` had no same-named test file anywhere on `origin/develop` at filing time (verified via `git ls-tree -r origin/develop` immediately before filing).

## How it tests

The concept's `build(THREE, TSL, U, parent)` already receives its WebGPU host as an injected parameter, so the suite drives the **real, unmodified module** through a deterministic in-memory host stand-in that records scene-graph wiring and state mutations. No `vi.mock`, no network, no WebGL. The fake only supplies constructible geometry/material/line classes and stores what the concept writes — every assertion exercises neon.ts's own logic.

Covered behaviour (12 cases):

- gallery registration contract (`id`/`label`/`family`)
- halo-behind-core parenting order and draw order (`renderOrder` 0 vs 1), transparent + `depthWrite=false` + NormalBlending material setup
- trefoil edge wiring: both edge geometries wrap the same `TorusKnotGeometry(0.68, 0.22, 128, 16, 2, 3)` knot but are distinct instances
- time-driven rotation of both layers (`y=t*0.14`, `x=sin(t*0.09)*0.18`, `z=cos(t*0.07)*0.08`)
- bass-coupled beat scale with preserved +4% halo offset (`1+low*0.12`, halo ×1.04)
- opacity clamps at exactly 1.0 (core) and 0.8 (halo) under extreme audio
- baseline glow at silent audio (core 0.85, halo 0.5 after any frame)
- hue-cycle gating: pinned at magenta/cyan while idle, including at the `respond === 0.05` boundary (strictly greater opens the gate)
- hue swing while responding: one easing step values and full convergence to swapped hues over repeated frames
- `listen` channel is inert for this concept
- dispose releases all three geometries and both materials and clears the parent group
- build independence: simultaneous builds share no state (the comparison harness builds several variants)

## Evidence

Local runs only — this comes from a fork, so hosted CI does not run on this PR.

- `vitest` 4.1.10, `packages/ui` config: **Test Files 1 passed (1); Tests 12 passed (12)** — re-run immediately before filing against working-tree content verified byte-identical to current `origin/develop` (`git diff origin/develop -- .../neon.ts .../orb-kit.ts` was empty).
- `biome check`: `Checked 1 file. No fixes applied.` (root config present, worktree not sparse).
- `tsc --noEmit` in `packages/ui`: grepping the output for the new filename yields zero matches — the file contributes no type diagnostics.

the deterministic proxy. Produce with:

```bash
  packages/scenario-runner/bin/eliza-scenarios run <scenario> --report <out.json>
```

Link the JSON report, run viewer, native jsonl, or write `N/A - <reason>`.

## Backend + frontend logs

Backend: structured logger lines ([ClassName] …) showing the code path firing end to end.
Frontend: console + network trace showing the request/response and state change.
Paste here inline (wrap long output in a `<details>` block), or write
`N/A - <reason>`.

## Screenshots (before / after) + video walkthrough

Full-page before AND after screenshots are required for any UI change. Include a
video click-through of the flow.

```bash
  bun run evidence:doctor                 (check capture tools; prints fixes for any missing)
  bun run test:e2e:record                 (general E2E recordings)
  bun run test:matrix:review              (capture into one verified evidence bundle)
  bun run evidence:review:no-open -- --bundle=evidence/runs/<run-id>
                                          (re-open the exact matrix run)
  bun run --cwd packages/app audit:app    (app + cloud UI — REQUIRED for UI changes)
```

The matrix command creates and verifies one named bundle, then passes that
exact run to the reviewer. Do not replace the `--bundle` path with raw producer
directories; `--source` is only for explicit archived/ad-hoc compatibility.

A UI-touching diff (rendered `.tsx`/`.css`/`.svg` under `packages/app`,
`packages/ui`, `apps/app`, …) MUST attach real screenshot/video/OCR artifacts —
the CI evidence gate rejects `N/A` on those rows for such diffs, label or not.
If a capture tool is missing, `evidence:doctor` prints the install command;
install it rather than marking evidence `N/A`.

7b:Tce9,# ElizaOS Overall Project Summary: Aug 25, 2026

Development on August 25 focused on hardening the computer-use framework and stabilizing core orchestration, voice, and media lifecycles. A major cross-plugin initiative was launched to ensure robust UTF-16 surrogate pair handling, alongside a project-wide enforcement of the canonical design system.

## ✅ Completed Work

### Computer-Use and Coding Tooling
*   **Hardened computer-use capabilities:** Implemented observation-bound sessions, cancellation fencing, and macOS accessibility controls ([elizaos/eliza#28389](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28389)).
*   **Refined coding action profiles:** Introduced a typed Pi-default profile with worktree opt-in and improved session durability ([elizaos/eliza#28287](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28287), [elizaos/eliza#28289](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28289), [elizaos/eliza#28408](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28408)).
*   **Improved hardware integration:** Added native `grim` screenshot support for Wayland/wlroots hosts ([elizaos/eliza#28444](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28444)).

### UI/UX and Design System
*   **Design system standardization:** Enforced a canonical design system across the application, including migration of raw controls and tables ([elizaos/eliza#28281](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28281)).
*   **Accessibility and testing:** Refactored shared UI molecules and fixed test resolution issues by aliasing cloud-sdk browser-contracts ([elizaos/eliza#28397](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28397), [elizaos/eliza#27853](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/27853)).

### Voice, Media, and Orchestration
*   **Streamlined voice responses:** Resolved stalls by routing authenticated phone turns through the conversation coordinator ([elizaos/eliza#28318](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28318), [elizaos/eliza#28327](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28327)).
*   **Enhanced security and stability:** Grounded media actions to prevent misinterpretations and added credential bridge caller authentication ([elizaos/eliza#28369](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28369), [elizaos/eliza#28325](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28325), [elizaos/eliza#28439](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28439), [elizaos/eliza#28440](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/28440)).

## 🏗️ Work in Progress
